           This file contains birth records from the parish
           of Glueckstal, South Russia for the decade 188x.
           This information was compiled by Dale Wahl and 
           coworkers from the St. Petersburg Lutheran Evangelical
           archives published by the LDS and edited by Marty
           McMahon.
